6XPPDU\
6XPPDU\
Strategy for Socio-Technical System Transition (4th Year)
͎Project Leader: Wichin SongSeong-Min Hong
͎Participants: Jieun SeongSueun KimKyu-young HanYuna LeeHee-Je
BakByung Kul JungHong-Tak Lim
The research of the "Strategy for Socio-Technical System Transition" aims to
reconstruct Korea’s sci ence and technology innovation policy based on
socio-technical system transition th eory. Beyond the existing industrial
innovation-oriented policies, we will look at the direction of science and
technology innovation policy from the viewpoint of social problem-solving and
system transition and perform a ne w interpretation of the policies. This will
reconstructe the science and technology innovation policy that has reached the
maturity stage, and will provide a momentum to transform the growth-oriented,
top-down, and supplier-centered innovation policy paradigms formed in the
existing system.
This fourth year of the study is composed of three parts. The first part deals
with the development direction of public research institutes from the perspective
of system transition as a policy research. In the second part, we analyze major
issues and trends related to social problem-solving research and social innovation
that are aimed at system transition. In Part 3, we discuss about the cases in the
field in terms of organizing the living lab and social problem-solving innovations
through collaborative interviews.
